
      function getDefaultPowerWatt(deviceType) {
        return defaultPowerWattByType[deviceType] ?? 0;
      }

      // Erst die Grundlast schätzen; CPU und GPU kommen extra dazu.
      const hardwareBasePowerWattByType = {
        // Grundlast ohne CPU/GPU
        'PC': 70, 'Mini-PC': 18, 'Laptop': 10, Server: 120,
        'Rack-Server': 150, 'Compute-Server': 180, 'GPU-Server': 250,
        'Storage-Server': 150, 'SAN-Array': 250, NAS: 35,
        'Server-Switch': 35, 'Core-Switch': 70, 'Fibre-Channel-Switch': 50,
        Router: 25, Firewall: 45, 'Load-Balancer': 60,
        'KVM-Switch': 15, 'Console-Server': 20, 'Backup-Server': 120,
        'Tape-Library': 100, 'Management-Server': 35
      };

      function estimateCpuPowerWatt(model, deviceType) {
        const name = String(model || '').toLowerCase().replace(/\(r\)|\(tm\)/g, '').trim();
        if (!name) return 0;
        const catalogCpuWatt = plannerWattFromSpec(getPlannerCpuHardwareSpec(model)?.power);
        if (catalogCpuWatt > 0) return catalogCpuWatt;

        // Mobile CPUs und die sparsamen x86-Modelle.
        if (/\b(?:intel )?atom\b|\bceleron n\d+|\bpentium silver\b|\bintel processor n\d+/i.test(name)) {
          const nMatch = name.match(/\bn(\d{3,4})\b/);
          return nMatch && Number(nMatch[1]) >= 200 ? 10 : 8;
        }
        if (/\b(?:snapdragon|qualcomm|apple a\d+|apple m[1-5]|mediatek|exynos|tegra|cortex|ampere altra|graviton)\b/i.test(name)) {
          if (/apple m[1-5].*ultra/.test(name)) return 70;
          if (/apple m[1-5].*max/.test(name)) return 45;
          if (/apple m[1-5].*pro/.test(name)) return 30;
          if (/apple m[1-5]/.test(name)) return 20;
          if (/snapdragon x elite/.test(name)) return 35;
          if (/snapdragon x plus/.test(name)) return 25;
          if (/snapdragon x/.test(name)) return 23;
          if (/graviton|ampere altra/.test(name)) return 180;
          return 12;
        }

        if (/\bthreadripper pro\b/.test(name)) return 280;
        if (/\bthreadripper\b/.test(name)) return 250;

        if (/\bepyc\b/.test(name)) {
          const modelMatch = name.match(/epyc\s+(\d{4,5})/);
          const id = modelMatch ? modelMatch[1] : '';
          const known = {
            '7251': 120, '7282': 120, '7302': 155, '7313': 155,
            '7352': 155, '7402': 200, '7443': 200, '7452': 155,
            '7453': 225, '7502': 200, '7543': 225, '7551': 180,
            '7552': 200, '7642': 225, '7662': 200, '7713': 225,
            '7742': 225, '7763': 280, '7773x': 280, '9174f': 320,
            '9224': 200, '9254': 200, '9274f': 320, '9334': 210,
            '9354': 280, '9374f': 320, '9454': 290, '9474f': 360,
            '9554': 360, '9654': 360, '9754': 360, '9845': 400,
            '9965': 500, '9995': 500
          };
          const fullId = (name.match(/epyc\s+(\d{4,5}[a-z]?)/) || [])[1] || id;
          if (known[fullId]) return known[fullId];
          if (/epyc embedded/.test(name)) return 35;
          if (/\b99\d\d\b/.test(name)) return 500;
          if (/\b9[678]\d\d\b/.test(name)) return 360;
          if (/\b9[345]\d\d\b/.test(name)) return 280;
          if (/\b9[12]\d\d\b/.test(name)) return 200;
          if (/\b7\d{3}\b/.test(name)) return 225;
          return 180;
        }

        if (/\bxeon phi\b/.test(name)) return 250;
        if (/\bxeon platinum\b/.test(name)) return 250;
        if (/\bxeon gold\b/.test(name)) return 210;
        if (/\bxeon silver\b/.test(name)) return 150;
        if (/\bxeon bronze\b/.test(name)) return 100;
        if (/\bxeon d-?\d/.test(name)) return 75;
        if (/\bxeon w-?\d/.test(name)) return 180;
        if (/\bxeon e-?\d/.test(name)) return 80;
        if (/\bxeon\b/.test(name)) {
          if (/e7-/.test(name)) return 130;
          if (/e5-26/.test(name)) return 120;
          if (/e5-16/.test(name)) return 95;
          return 100;
        }

        if (/\bryzen ai max\+?\s*395\b/.test(name)) return 120;
        if (/\bryzen ai\b/.test(name)) return 45;
        if (/\bryzen\b/.test(name)) {
          if (/\b\d{3,5}hx\b/.test(name)) return 75;
          if (/\b\d{3,5}hs\b/.test(name)) return 45;
          if (/\b\d{3,5}h\b/.test(name)) return 45;
          if (/\b\d{3,5}u\b/.test(name)) return 20;
          if (/\b\d{3,5}p\b/.test(name)) return 28;
          if (/\b\d{3,5}y\b/.test(name)) return 8;
          if (/\b[79]\d{3,4}x3d\b/.test(name)) return /ryzen 9/.test(name) ? 170 : 120;
          if (/\bryzen 9\b/.test(name)) return /\b(?:7900|9900)x\b/.test(name) ? 120 : 170;
          if (/\bryzen 7\b/.test(name)) return /\b(?:5800x|7700x|9700x|1800x|2700x|3800x)\b/.test(name) ? 105 : 65;
          if (/\bryzen 5\b/.test(name)) return /\b\d{4}x\b/.test(name) ? 95 : 65;
          if (/\bryzen 3\b/.test(name)) return 35;
          return 65;
        }

        if (/\b(?:fx-9590|fx-9370)\b/.test(name)) return 220;
        if (/\bfx-8\d{3}\b/.test(name)) return 125;
        if (/\bfx-6\d{3}\b/.test(name)) return 95;
        if (/\bfx-\d+/.test(name)) return 65;
        if (/\bathlon silver .*\b\d{4}u\b/.test(name)) return 15;
        if (/\bathlon\b/.test(name)) return /\b200ge\b|\b220ge\b|\b3000g\b/.test(name) ? 35 : 65;
        if (/\bamd a4\b/.test(name)) return 65;
        if (/\bamd a6\b|\bamd a8\b/.test(name)) return 95;
        if (/\bamd a10\b/.test(name)) return 95;
        if (/\bopteron\b/.test(name)) return 115;

        // Intel: am Suffix sieht man Desktop vs. Mobil
        if (/\b(?:core ultra|core [3579])\b/.test(name)) {
          if (/\b\d{3,4}u\b/.test(name)) return 15;
          if (/\b\d{3,4}v\b/.test(name)) return 25;
          if (/\b\d{3,4}p\b/.test(name)) return 28;
          if (/\b\d{3,4}hx\b/.test(name)) return 55;
          if (/\b\d{3,4}hk\b|\b\d{3,4}h\b/.test(name)) return 45;
          if (/\bi[3579]-\d{4,5}g\d\b/.test(name)) return 28;
        }
        if (/\bcore ultra [3579]\b/.test(name)) {
          if (/\b\d{3,4}k\b/.test(name)) return 125;
          return 65;
        }
        if (/\bcore i9\b/.test(name)) {
          if (/\b\d{4,5}t\b/.test(name)) return 35;
          if (/\b\d{4,5}hx\b/.test(name)) return 55;
          if (/\b\d{4,5}h\b/.test(name)) return 45;
          if (/\b\d{4,5}u\b/.test(name)) return 15;
          if (/\b\d{4,5}k[sf]?\b/.test(name)) return 125;
          return 65;
        }
        if (/\bcore i7\b/.test(name)) {
          if (/\b\d{4,5}t\b/.test(name)) return 35;
          if (/\b\d{4,5}hx\b/.test(name)) return 55;
          if (/\b\d{4,5}h[qk]?\b/.test(name)) return 45;
          if (/\b\d{4,5}u\b/.test(name)) return 15;
          if (/\b\d{4,5}p\b/.test(name)) return 28;
          if (/\b\d{4,5}k[f]?\b/.test(name)) return 125;
          if (/\bi7-\d{4,5}g\d\b/.test(name)) return 28;
          return 65;
        }
        if (/\bcore i5\b/.test(name)) {
          if (/\b\d{4,5}t\b/.test(name)) return 35;
          if (/\b\d{4,5}hx\b/.test(name)) return 55;
          if (/\b\d{4,5}h[qk]?\b/.test(name)) return 45;
          if (/\b\d{4,5}u\b/.test(name)) return 15;
          if (/\b\d{4,5}p\b/.test(name)) return 28;
          if (/\b\d{4,5}k[f]?\b/.test(name)) return 125;
          return 65;
        }
        if (/\bcore i3\b/.test(name)) {
          if (/\b\d{4,5}u\b/.test(name)) return 15;
          if (/\b\d{4,5}t\b/.test(name)) return 35;
          if (/\b\d{4,5}k\b/.test(name)) return 91;
          return 35;
        }
        if (/\bpentium\b|\bceleron\b/.test(name)) return 35;
        if (/\bintel n\d{3,4}\b/.test(name)) return 8;
        if (/\bintel core\b/.test(name)) return 65;
        if (/\barm\b/.test(name)) return 12;
        return /laptop|mobile|notebook/.test(name) || deviceType === 'Laptop' ? 25 : 65;
      }

      function estimateGpuPowerWatt(model, deviceType) {
        const name = String(model || '').toLowerCase().trim();
        if (!name) return 0;
        let watts = 0;
        const catalogGpuWatt = plannerWattFromSpec(getPlannerGpuHardwareSpec(model)?.tdp);
        if (catalogGpuWatt > 0) return catalogGpuWatt;

        // Rechenzentrum / KI-Karten
        if (/gb200 nvl72/.test(name)) return 120000;
        if (/\bgb300\b/.test(name)) watts = 1200;
        else if (/\bb200\b/.test(name)) watts = 1000;
        else if (/\bb100\b/.test(name)) watts = 700;
        else if (/\bh200\b/.test(name)) watts = 700;
        else if (/\bh100\b|\bh800\b/.test(name)) watts = 700;
        else if (/\bh20\b/.test(name)) watts = 400;
        else if (/\ba800\b|\ba100\b/.test(name)) watts = 400;
        else if (/\ba40\b/.test(name)) watts = 300;
        else if (/\ba30\b/.test(name)) watts = 165;
        else if (/\ba16\b/.test(name)) watts = 250;
        else if (/\ba2\b/.test(name)) watts = 60;
        else if (/\bl40s\b/.test(name)) watts = 350;
        else if (/\bl40\b/.test(name)) watts = 300;
        else if (/\bl4\b/.test(name)) watts = 72;
        else if (/\btesla k80\b/.test(name)) watts = 300;
        else if (/\btesla v100\b/.test(name)) watts = 300;
        else if (/\btesla p100\b/.test(name)) watts = 250;
        else if (/\btesla p40\b|\btesla m40\b/.test(name)) watts = 250;
        else if (/\btesla t4\b/.test(name)) watts = 70;
        else if (/\btesla p4\b/.test(name)) watts = 75;
        else if (/\btrainium|\binferentia|\btpu v[2-6]|\bascend 910/.test(name)) watts = 300;

        // GeForce RTX
        else if (/\brtx\s*5090\b/.test(name)) watts = 575;
        else if (/\brtx\s*5080\b/.test(name)) watts = 360;
        else if (/\brtx\s*5070\s*ti\b/.test(name)) watts = 300;
        else if (/\brtx\s*5070\b/.test(name)) watts = 250;
        else if (/\brtx\s*5060\s*ti\b/.test(name)) watts = 180;
        else if (/\brtx\s*5060\b/.test(name)) watts = 145;
        else if (/\brtx\s*5050\b/.test(name)) watts = 100;
        else if (/\brtx\s*4090\b/.test(name)) watts = 450;
        else if (/\brtx\s*4080\b/.test(name)) watts = 320;
        else if (/\brtx\s*4070\s*ti/.test(name)) watts = 285;
        else if (/\brtx\s*4070\s*super\b/.test(name)) watts = 220;
        else if (/\brtx\s*4070\b/.test(name)) watts = 200;
        else if (/\brtx\s*4060\s*ti\b/.test(name)) watts = 160;
        else if (/\brtx\s*4060\b/.test(name)) watts = 115;
        else if (/\brtx\s*4050\b/.test(name)) watts = 80;
        else if (/\brtx\s*3090\s*ti\b/.test(name)) watts = 450;
        else if (/\brtx\s*3090\b/.test(name)) watts = 350;
        else if (/\brtx\s*3080\s*ti\b/.test(name)) watts = 350;
        else if (/\brtx\s*3080\b/.test(name)) watts = 320;
        else if (/\brtx\s*3070\s*ti\b/.test(name)) watts = 290;
        else if (/\brtx\s*3070\b/.test(name)) watts = 220;
        else if (/\brtx\s*3060\s*ti\b/.test(name)) watts = 200;
        else if (/\brtx\s*3060\b/.test(name)) watts = 170;
        else if (/\brtx\s*3050\b/.test(name)) watts = 130;
        else if (/\brtx\s*2080\s*ti\b/.test(name)) watts = 250;
        else if (/\brtx\s*2080\s*super\b/.test(name)) watts = 250;
        else if (/\brtx\s*2080\b/.test(name)) watts = 215;
        else if (/\brtx\s*2070\s*super\b/.test(name)) watts = 215;
        else if (/\brtx\s*2070\b/.test(name)) watts = 175;
        else if (/\brtx\s*2060\s*super\b/.test(name)) watts = 175;
        else if (/\brtx\s*2060\b/.test(name)) watts = 160;

        // Workstation (Quadro / RTX)
        else if (/\brtx pro 6000 blackwell\b/.test(name) || /\brtx 6000 blackwell\b/.test(name)) watts = 600;
        else if (/\brtx pro 5000 blackwell\b/.test(name)) watts = 300;
        else if (/\brtx pro 4000 blackwell\b/.test(name)) watts = 200;
        else if (/\brtx 6000 ada\b|\brtx a6000\b|\bquadro rtx 6000\b|\bquadro rtx 8000\b/.test(name)) watts = 300;
        else if (/\brtx 5000 ada\b|\brtx a5000\b|\bquadro rtx 5000\b/.test(name)) watts = 230;
        else if (/\brtx 4500 ada\b/.test(name)) watts = 210;
        else if (/\brtx 4000 ada\b|\brtx a4000\b|\bquadro rtx 4000\b/.test(name)) watts = 140;
        else if (/\brtx 2000(?:e)? ada\b|\brtx a2000\b/.test(name)) watts = 70;
        else if (/\brtx a1000\b|\brtx pro 2000 blackwell\b/.test(name)) watts = 50;
        else if (/\bquadro p6000\b/.test(name)) watts = 250;
        else if (/\bquadro p5000\b/.test(name)) watts = 180;
        else if (/\bquadro p4000\b/.test(name)) watts = 105;
        else if (/\bquadro p2000\b/.test(name)) watts = 75;
        else if (/\bquadro p1000\b/.test(name)) watts = 47;
        else if (/\bquadro k6000\b/.test(name)) watts = 225;
        else if (/\bquadro k[245]000\b/.test(name)) watts = 100;
        else if (/\bquadro m6000\b/.test(name)) watts = 250;
        else if (/\bquadro m[245]000\b/.test(name)) watts = 120;

        // GTX / GT
        else if (/\bgtx\s*titan z\b/.test(name)) watts = 375;
        else if (/\bgtx\s*(?:titan x|titan black|titan)\b/.test(name)) watts = 250;
        else if (/\bgtx\s*1080\s*ti\b/.test(name)) watts = 250;
        else if (/\bgtx\s*1080\b/.test(name)) watts = 180;
        else if (/\bgtx\s*1070\s*ti\b/.test(name)) watts = 180;
        else if (/\bgtx\s*1070\b/.test(name)) watts = 150;
        else if (/\bgtx\s*1660\s*ti\b/.test(name)) watts = 120;
        else if (/\bgtx\s*1660\s*super\b/.test(name)) watts = 125;
        else if (/\bgtx\s*1660\b/.test(name)) watts = 120;
        else if (/\bgtx\s*1650\s*super\b/.test(name)) watts = 100;
        else if (/\bgtx\s*1650\b|\bgtx\s*1630\b/.test(name)) watts = 75;
        else if (/\bgtx\s*1060\b/.test(name)) watts = 120;
        else if (/\bgtx\s*1050\s*ti\b/.test(name)) watts = 75;
        else if (/\bgtx\s*1050\b/.test(name)) watts = 75;
        else if (/\bgtx\s*980\s*ti\b/.test(name)) watts = 250;
        else if (/\bgtx\s*980\b/.test(name)) watts = 165;
        else if (/\bgtx\s*970\b/.test(name)) watts = 145;
        else if (/\bgtx\s*960\b/.test(name)) watts = 120;
        else if (/\bgtx\s*950\b/.test(name)) watts = 90;
        else if (/\bgtx\s*780(?:\s*ti)?\b/.test(name)) watts = 250;
        else if (/\bgtx\s*770\b/.test(name)) watts = 230;
        else if (/\bgtx\s*760\b/.test(name)) watts = 170;
        else if (/\bgtx\s*750\s*ti\b/.test(name)) watts = 60;
        else if (/\bgtx\s*750\b/.test(name)) watts = 55;
        else if (/\bgtx\s*6[456]\d\b/.test(name)) watts = 75;
        else if (/\bgtx\s*5\d\d\b/.test(name)) watts = 150;
        else if (/\bgt\s*710\b|\bgt\s*720\b/.test(name)) watts = 20;
        else if (/\bgt\s*730\b/.test(name)) watts = 38;
        else if (/\bgt\s*740\b/.test(name)) watts = 64;
        else if (/\bgt\s*1010\b|\bgt\s*1030\b/.test(name)) watts = 30;

        // AMD Radeon
        else if (/\brx\s*9070\s*xt\b/.test(name)) watts = 304;
        else if (/\brx\s*9070\s*gre\b/.test(name)) watts = 220;
        else if (/\brx\s*9070\b/.test(name)) watts = 220;
        else if (/\brx\s*9060\s*xt\b/.test(name)) watts = 160;
        else if (/\brx\s*9060\b/.test(name)) watts = 130;
        else if (/\brx\s*8900\s*xtx\b/.test(name)) watts = 355;
        else if (/\brx\s*8800\s*xt\b/.test(name)) watts = 300;
        else if (/\brx\s*8700\s*xt\b/.test(name)) watts = 250;
        else if (/\brx\s*7900\s*xtx\b/.test(name)) watts = 355;
        else if (/\brx\s*7900\s*xt\b/.test(name)) watts = 315;
        else if (/\brx\s*7900\s*gre\b/.test(name)) watts = 260;
        else if (/\brx\s*7800\s*xt\b/.test(name)) watts = 263;
        else if (/\brx\s*7700\s*xt\b/.test(name)) watts = 245;
        else if (/\brx\s*7600\s*xt\b/.test(name)) watts = 190;
        else if (/\brx\s*7600\b/.test(name)) watts = 165;
        else if (/\brx\s*6950\s*xt\b/.test(name)) watts = 335;
        else if (/\brx\s*6900\s*xt\b/.test(name)) watts = 300;
        else if (/\brx\s*6800\s*xt\b/.test(name)) watts = 300;
        else if (/\brx\s*6800\b/.test(name)) watts = 250;
        else if (/\brx\s*6750\s*xt\b/.test(name)) watts = 250;
        else if (/\brx\s*6700\s*xt\b/.test(name)) watts = 230;
        else if (/\brx\s*6650\s*xt\b/.test(name)) watts = 180;
        else if (/\brx\s*6600\s*xt\b/.test(name)) watts = 160;
        else if (/\brx\s*6600\b/.test(name)) watts = 132;
        else if (/\brx\s*6500\s*xt\b/.test(name)) watts = 107;
        else if (/\brx\s*580\b/.test(name)) watts = 185;
        else if (/\brx\s*570\b/.test(name)) watts = 150;
        else if (/\brx\s*560\b/.test(name)) watts = 80;
        else if (/\brx\s*480\b/.test(name)) watts = 150;
        else if (/\brx\s*470\b/.test(name)) watts = 120;
        else if (/\brx\s*vega\s*64\b/.test(name)) watts = 295;
        else if (/\brx\s*vega\s*56\b/.test(name)) watts = 210;
        else if (/\bradeon vii\b/.test(name)) watts = 300;
        else if (/\bradeon pro w7900\b/.test(name)) watts = 295;
        else if (/\bradeon pro w7800\b/.test(name)) watts = 260;
        else if (/\bradeon pro w6800\b/.test(name)) watts = 250;
        else if (/\bradeon pro w6600\b/.test(name)) watts = 130;
        else if (/\binstinct mi300x\b/.test(name)) watts = 750;
        else if (/\binstinct mi250\b|\binstinct mi250x\b/.test(name)) watts = 500;
        else if (/\binstinct mi100\b/.test(name)) watts = 300;
        else if (/\bfirepro s10000\b/.test(name)) watts = 375;
        else if (/\bfirepro s9150\b/.test(name)) watts = 235;
        else if (/\bfirepro s9000\b/.test(name)) watts = 225;
        else if (/\bfirepro\b|\bradeon pro\b/.test(name)) watts = 120;

        // Intel Arc
        else if (/\barc b580\b/.test(name)) watts = 190;
        else if (/\barc b570\b/.test(name)) watts = 150;
        else if (/\barc a770\b/.test(name)) watts = 225;
        else if (/\barc a750\b/.test(name)) watts = 225;
        else if (/\barc a580\b/.test(name)) watts = 185;
        else if (/\barc a380\b/.test(name)) watts = 75;
        else if (/\barc a310\b/.test(name)) watts = 50;
        else if (/\barc pro b70\b/.test(name)) watts = 200;
        else if (/\barc pro b60\b/.test(name)) watts = 150;
        else if (/\barc pro|data center gpu flex|data center gpu max/.test(name)) watts = 150;

        // iGPUs und kleine Mobil-GPUs ziehen kaum Strom
        else if (/apple .*gpu/.test(name)) {
          if (/ultra/.test(name)) watts = 70;
          else if (/max/.test(name)) watts = 45;
          else if (/pro/.test(name)) watts = 30;
          else watts = 18;
        }
        else if (/intel hd graphics|intel uhd graphics|iris graphics|iris xe|arc graphics|adreno|mali|immortalis/.test(name)) watts = 15;
        else if (/radeon graphics|radeon vega (?:[3568]\b|\d+ graphics)/.test(name)) watts = 25;
        else if (/\bquadro\b/.test(name)) watts = 100;
        else if (/nvidia/.test(name)) watts = 180;
        else if (/\bradeon rx\b/.test(name)) watts = 180;
        else watts = 35;

        if (/laptop|mobile|max-q|max q|\b\d{3,4}m\b/.test(name)) watts = Math.max(20, Math.round(watts * 0.5));
        return watts;
      }

      function estimateSelectedHardwarePowerWatt(deviceType, cpuModel, gpuModel, gpuCount = 1) {
        const cpu = String(cpuModel || '').trim();
        const gpu = String(gpuModel || '').trim();
        if (!cpu && !gpu) return getDefaultPowerWatt(deviceType);
        const base = hardwareBasePowerWattByType[deviceType] ?? Math.max(15, Math.round(getDefaultPowerWatt(deviceType) * 0.2));
        const count = gpu ? clamp(Math.floor(Number(gpuCount) || 1), 1, 16) : 0;
        const gpuPower = gpu ? estimateGpuPowerWatt(gpu, deviceType) * count : 0;
        return Math.round(base + estimateCpuPowerWatt(cpu, deviceType) + gpuPower);
      }

      function updatePowerEstimateFromHardware() {
        const deviceType = ui.deviceType.value;
        const cpuModel = hardwareSpecTypes.has(deviceType) ? ui.cpuModel.value : '';
        const gpuModel = gpuSpecTypes.has(deviceType) ? ui.gpuModel.value : '';
        const gpuCount = ui.gpuCount ? ui.gpuCount.value : 1;
        ui.devicePowerWatt.value = estimateSelectedHardwarePowerWatt(deviceType, cpuModel, gpuModel, gpuCount);
        ui.devicePowerWatt.dataset.customized = 'false';
      }

      function hasPowerInput(ports, powerWatt) {
        return ports.some((port) => isPowerPort(port.type)) || (
          Number(powerWatt) <= 100 && ports.some((port) => (port.type || '').trim().toLowerCase() === 'lan')
        );
      }

      function calculateTotalPower() {
        let total = 0;
        state.devices.forEach((device) => {
          const watt = Number(device.powerWatt) || 0;
          total += watt;
        });
        return total;
      }

      function getConnectedEndpoint(device, port) {
        const cable = port.cableId ? state.cables.get(port.cableId) : null;
        if (!cable) return null;
        if (cable.fromDeviceId === device.id && cable.fromPortId === port.id) {
          const otherDevice = getDeviceById(cable.toDeviceId);
          return otherDevice ? { device: otherDevice, port: getPortById(otherDevice.id, cable.toPortId) } : null;
        }
        if (cable.toDeviceId === device.id && cable.toPortId === port.id) {
          const otherDevice = getDeviceById(cable.fromDeviceId);
          return otherDevice ? { device: otherDevice, port: getPortById(otherDevice.id, cable.fromPortId) } : null;
        }
        return null;
      }

      function calculateDeviceLoad(device, visited = new Set()) {
        if (!device) return 0;
        if (!powerDistributionTypes.has(device.type)) return Number(device.powerWatt) || 0;
        if (visited.has(device.id)) return 0;
        visited.add(device.id);
        const total = device.ports
          .filter((port) => port.powerRole === 'outlet')
          .reduce((sum, port) => sum + calculateOutletLoad(device, port, visited), 0);
        visited.delete(device.id);
        return total;
      }

      function calculateOutletLoad(device, port, visited = new Set()) {
        const endpoint = getConnectedEndpoint(device, port);
        return endpoint ? calculateDeviceLoad(endpoint.device, visited) : 0;
      }

      function renderPowerBar() {
        const total = calculateTotalPower();
        const percent = clamp((total / state.maxPowerWatt) * 100, 0, 100);
        const warning = total > state.maxPowerWatt;
        ui.powerFill.style.width = `${percent}%`;
        ui.powerLabel.textContent = `${t('power')}: ${total} / ${state.maxPowerWatt} W`;
        ui.powerWrap.classList.toggle('warning', warning);
        if (warning) {
          ui.powerLabel.title = t('warnOverLimit');
        } else {
          ui.powerLabel.title = 'OK';
        }
      }

      function populateTypeSelect() {
        const selectedType = ui.deviceType.value;
        ui.deviceType.innerHTML = '';
        deviceTypes.forEach((type) => {
          const option = document.createElement('option');
          option.value = type;
          option.textContent = getDeviceTypeLabel(type);
          ui.deviceType.appendChild(option);
        });
        ui.deviceType.value = selectedType;
      }

      function populateCableTypeSelect() {
        ui.cableTypeSelect.innerHTML = '';
        cableTypes.forEach((type) => {
          const option = document.createElement('option');
          option.value = type;
          option.textContent = getCableTypeLabel(type);
          ui.cableTypeSelect.appendChild(option);
        });
        ui.cableTypeSelect.value = state.selectedCableType;
      }

VRAM, TDP und Erscheinungsjahr nicht einzeln bestätigt und deshalb nicht geschätzt","","","","","","","https://www.techpowerup.com/gpu-specs/",""]];
      const plannerCpuSpecsByExactName = Object.create(null);
      const plannerGpuSpecsByExactName = Object.create(null);
      const plannerCpuSpecsByNormalizedName = Object.create(null);
      const plannerGpuSpecsByNormalizedName = Object.create(null);
      
      function normalizePlannerHardwareName(value) {
        return String(value || '').trim().toLocaleLowerCase()
          .replace(/\(r\)|\(tm\)|®|™/gi, '')
          .replace(/[^a-z0-9]+/g, '');
      }
      
      function addPlannerHardwareRow(exactMap, normalizedMap, name, spec) {
        const exact = String(name || '').trim().toLocaleLowerCase();
        if (!exact) return;
        if (!Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(exactMap, exact)) exactMap[exact] = spec;
        const normalized = normalizePlannerHardwareName(name);
        if (!normalized) return;
        if (!Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(normalizedMap, normalized)) normalizedMap[normalized] = spec;
        else if (normalizedMap[normalized] && normalizedMap[normalized].name !== spec.name) normalizedMap[normalized] = null;
      }
      
      plannerCpuHardwareRows.forEach((row) => {
        const spec = {
          name: row[0], cores: row[1], threads: row[2], baseClock: row[3], boostClock: row[4],
          year: row[5], architecture: row[6], power: row[7], source: row[8],
          socket: row[9], process: row[10], graphics: row[11], memory: row[12]
        };
        addPlannerHardwareRow(plannerCpuSpecsByExactName, plannerCpuSpecsByNormalizedName, spec.name, spec);
      });
      
      plannerGpuHardwareRows.forEach((row) => {
        const spec = {
          name: row[0], vramInfo: row[1], tdp: row[2], yearInfo: row[3], dataStatus: row[4],
          baseClockMHz: row[5], boostClockMHz: row[6], computeUnits: row[7],
          memoryType: row[8], architecture: row[9], memoryBandwidth: row[10], source: row[11], specialNotes: row[12]
        };
        addPlannerHardwareRow(plannerGpuSpecsByExactName, plannerGpuSpecsByNormalizedName, spec.name, spec);
      });
